    <div class="comment-body markdown-body js-comment-body soft-wrap user-select-contain d-block">
      <p dir="auto">Expose keep alive related timeout values, so they can be configured individually.</p>
<p dir="auto">We are concurrently communicating with a Mainframe, that closes the connections after 10 seconds, so we need to be able to configure this on the client side, to avoid any "Connection was closed" errors.</p>
<p dir="auto">I looked at this commit <a class="commit-link" href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/commit/fc0a9b3a7ffce6b8cbc7be35f30f6274df263253#diff-02109b73d940f5a3ec39ed8014c7da1e2dfe291ed458bf2a92e7785789aa3512"><tt>fc0a9b3</tt>#diff-02109b73d940f5a3ec39ed8014c7da1e2dfe291ed458bf2a92e7785789aa3512</a>, but could not find any tests related to this, so i have not created any.</p>
<p dir="auto">Please guide me if you think this is possible</p>
    </div>
  </task-lists>
  
</div>

      </div>

  <clipboard-copy aria-label="Copy link" for="issuecomment-3588613320-permalink" role="menuitem" data-dd-action-name="comment.copy_link" data-view-component="true" class="dropdown-item btn-link">

</span>            <span data-view-component="true">
  <clipboard-copy role="menuitem" data-dd-action-name="comment.copy_markdown" value="Here is the idea, how the test could look like: 

```java
package io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client;

import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

import jakarta.annotation.Resource;
import jakarta.jws.WebMethod;
import jakarta.jws.WebService;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.xml.ws.WebServiceContext;
import jakarta.xml.ws.handler.MessageContext;

import org.assertj.core.api.Assertions;
import org.jboss.shrinkwrap.api.ShrinkWrap;
import org.jboss.shrinkwrap.api.spec.JavaArchive;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.RegisterExtension;

import io.quarkiverse.cxf.annotation.CXFClient;
import io.quarkus.test.QuarkusUnitTest;

public class KeepAliveTest {

    @RegisterExtension
    public static final QuarkusUnitTest test = new QuarkusUnitTest()
            .setArchiveProducer(() -&gt; ShrinkWrap.create(JavaArchive.class)
                    .addClasses(EchoHeadersService.class, EchoHeadersServiceImpl.class))
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.endpoint.\&quot;/echoHeaders\&quot;.implementor&quot;, EchoHeadersServiceImpl.class.getName())
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.endpoint.\&quot;/echoHeaders\&quot;.logging.enabled&quot;, &quot;pretty&quot;)

            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.keepAliveClient.client-endpoint-url&quot;,
                    &quot;http://localhost:8081/services/echoHeaders&quot;)
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.keepAliveClient.service-interface&quot;, EchoHeadersService.class.getName())
            /* This scenario works only with vert.x client */
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.keepAliveClient.http-conduit-factory&quot;, &quot;VertxHttpClientHTTPConduitFactory&quot;)
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.keepAliveClient.connection&quot;, &quot;keep-alive&quot;)
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.keepAliveClient.vertx.keep-alive-timeout&quot;, &quot;123&quot;)

            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.closeClient.client-endpoint-url&quot;,
                    &quot;http://localhost:8081/services/echoHeaders&quot;)
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.closeClient.service-interface&quot;, EchoHeadersService.class.getName())
            /* This scenario works only with vert.x client */
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.closeClient.http-conduit-factory&quot;, &quot;VertxHttpClientHTTPConduitFactory&quot;)
            .overrideConfigKey(&quot;quarkus.cxf.client.closeClient.connection&quot;, &quot;close&quot;);

    @CXFClient(&quot;keepAliveClient&quot;)
    EchoHeadersService keepAliveClient;

    @CXFClient(&quot;closeClient&quot;)
    EchoHeadersService closeClient;

    @Test
    void keepAliveTimeout() {
        Assertions.assertThat(
                keepAliveClient.getRequestHeaders(&quot;connection,keep-alive&quot;))
                .isEqualTo(&quot;connection:Keep-Alive,keep-alive:timeout=123&quot;);
    }

    @Test
    void closeClient() {
        Assertions.assertThat(
                keepAliveClient.getRequestHeaders(&quot;connection&quot;))
                .isEqualTo(&quot;connection:Close&quot;);
    }

    @WebService
    public interface EchoHeadersService {

        @WebMethod
        String getRequestHeaders(String keys);

    }

    @WebService(serviceName = &quot;EchoHeaders&quot;)
    public static class EchoHeadersServiceImpl implements EchoHeadersService {

        @Resource
        WebServiceContext wsContext;

        @Override
        public String getRequestHeaders(String keys) {
            HttpServletRequest req = (HttpServletRequest) wsContext.getMessageContext().get(MessageContext.SERVLET_REQUEST);

            return Stream.of(keys.split(&quot;,&quot;))
                    .map(k -&gt; k + &quot;:&quot; + req.getHeader(k))
                    .collect(Collectors.joining(&quot;,&quot;));
        }
    }
}
```

You may want to add it under `extensions/core/deployment/src/test/java/io/quarkiverse/cxf/vertx/http/client`.

It would be good to cover also the HTTP/2 scenario." data-view-component="true" class="dropdown-item btn-link">
      
              Copy Markdown

</clipboard-copy>  <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true" class="sr-only" data-clipboard-copy-feedback></div>
</span>        </details-menu>

    <span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-k">static</span> <span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-smi">EchoHeadersServiceImpl</span> <span class="pl-k">implements</span> <span class="pl-smi">EchoHeadersService</span> {

        <span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">Resource</span>
        <span class="pl-smi">WebServiceContext</span> <span class="pl-s1">wsContext</span>;

        <span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">Override</span>
        <span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-en">getRequestHeaders</span>(<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">keys</span>) {
            <span class="pl-smi">HttpServletRequest</span> <span class="pl-s1">req</span> = (<span class="pl-smi">HttpServletRequest</span>) <span class="pl-s1">wsContext</span>.<span class="pl-en">getMessageContext</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-smi">MessageContext</span>.<span class="pl-c1">SERVLET_REQUEST</span>);

            <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-smi">Stream</span>.<span class="pl-en">of</span>(<span class="pl-s1">keys</span>.<span class="pl-en">split</span>(<span class="pl-s">","</span>))
                    .<span class="pl-en">map</span>(<span class="pl-s1">k</span> -&gt; <span class="pl-s1">k</span> + <span class="pl-s">":"</span> + <span class="pl-s1">req</span>.<span class="pl-en">getHeader</span>(<span class="pl-s1">k</span>))
                    .<span class="pl-en">collect</span>(<span class="pl-smi">Collectors</span>.<span class="pl-en">joining</span>(<span class="pl-s">","</span>));
        }
    }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You may want to add it under <code class="notranslate">extensions/core/deployment/src/test/java/io/quarkiverse/cxf/vertx/http/client</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">It would be good to cover also the HTTP/2 scenario.</p>

      </td>
    </tr>
  </tbody>
</table>
</task-lists>
